With no user message waiting at the CLI it does nothing: a message the running turn has already taken in, or was started for, is being answered. With one waiting that is not in the queue yet (received and still being admitted, or already taken for the next turn), that the running turn is taking in at that moment, or with no turn under way, it also does nothing: the message runs when its place comes.
